OpenCivil linear static and modal verification

100 structural models, each solved for linear static response and natural modes. Every model has its own .mf input, independent known solution, solver output, logs and numerical comparison.

100shared models
100 / 100static pass
100 / 100modal pass

The reference uses independent force-flexibility assembly and a dense symmetric eigenproblem. Textbook continuum solutions are recorded separately; modal comparisons account for the application's lumped translational mass, artificial rotational mass and release regularization. Expand a case for exact errors and tolerances.

Method and sources · Text report · CSV · Full JSON
